They do not provide a representation-carried token state that is non-participating at the attention boundary. We assign each token hidden carrier \\(h_i\\) an active-presence coefficient \\(p_i=\\lVert h_i\\rVert^2/(τ+\\lVert h_i\\rVert^2)\\). The same coefficient has two roles: it gates information emitted by token \\(i\\), and it determines the mass with which token \\(i\\) enters computations shared with oth","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T14:43:45Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21174.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作讨论 attention 模型里的零向量 token 是否能成为真正“不参与计算”的状态。标准 attention mask 控制的是 query-source 关系，但零向量本身并不会自然惰性化：零 query 会得到可见 value 的平均，零 key/value source 仍会向 softmax denominator 加入 exp(0)。","importance":"对 LLM 预训练的启发是架构边界条件。padding、空 latent slot、稀疏路由槽、缺失模态 token、可变长度 batch 都需要一种状态级 NULL，而不是只依赖关系级 mask。工作给出一个比较干净的算子约束：零向量 token 在 attention、norm、FFN、注入和统计操作中都不应影响已有 token。它还没有证明大规模 LLM 训练收益，因此更适合泛读，作为设计 masked/latent/multimodal 架构时的算子参考。","technical_approach":"核心是给每个 hidden carrier h_i 定义 active-presence 系数 p_i = ||h_i||^2 / (tau + ||h_i||^2)。同一个 p_i 承担两个角色：作为 receiver 时，用 p_i 门控该 token 的输出；作为 source 时，用 p_j 调整它进入 attention numerator 和 partition 的质量。这样零向量的 p 为 0，不发出输出，也不向共享 support 增加质量。OAttention 保留标准点积打分、外部 visibility mask、指数竞争和 value 聚合，但把 token presence 和 relation mask 分离。工作进一步提出 O-Closure：局部算子需要吸收零输入，support-coupled 算子需要给零 source 分配零质量。OFFN、ONorm、OInject、OStandardize 分别处理 FFN、归一化、位置或外部注入、共享统计等路径，并通过 residual addition 和 composition 构造条件性的 OTransformer。","experiments":"实验分三类。第一，有限精度下验证 OAttention 等算子满足声明的 null consistency，包括 null receiver 输出为零、插入 null source 不改变旧输出、self-attention 插入零 carrier 后旧 token 不变且新 token 输出为零、empty support 返回有限零输出。第二，在冻结的 pretrained TabPFN v3 host 中测试近似恒等兼容性，说明这些替换可以在受控条件下接入已有模型而不立即破坏行为。第三，做 scoped OTransformer 消融，显示只改 attention 不够，整条路径都满足 O-Closure 才能稳定保持零向量惰性。工作没有给出大规模语言模型预训练结果，也没有覆盖 tokenizer、router、cache、readout 的通用闭包。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20–30 分钟。先看引言里对标准 attention 零向量失败模式的说明，这部分最直接。然后看 null consistency 的四个定义和 OAttention 公式，确认 presence 同时放在 receiver output 和 source mass 上的原因。若关心架构实现，再看 O-Closure 中 OFFN、ONorm、OInject、OStandardize 的条件。实验部分只需看 scoped OTransformer 消融，重点是“只修 attention 不够”这个结论。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work asks whether a zero-vector token can be made truly inert in an attention model. Standard masks control allowed query-source relations, but they do not make the zero vector a non-participating state. A zero query still returns an average over visible values, and a zero key-value source still contributes exp(0) to the softmax denominator.","importance":"The relevance to foundation-model research is mostly architectural. Padding, empty latent slots, sparse routing positions, missing modality tokens, and variable-support computation all need a state-level NULL in addition to relation-level masks. The work gives a precise operator contract for this NULL behavior: a zero carrier should emit no update and should add no support mass to shared computations. It is not yet evidence for improved large-scale LLM pretraining, but it is a useful design reference for masked, latent, sparse, or multimodal models.","technical_approach":"Each hidden carrier h_i is assigned a smooth presence coefficient p_i = ||h_i||^2 / (tau + ||h_i||^2). The same coefficient is used in two places. As a receiver gate, p_i multiplies the token’s output. As a source mass, p_j weights that source in both the attention numerator and the partition function. OAttention keeps standard dot-product scoring, external visibility masks, exponential competition, and value aggregation, but separates token presence from relation visibility. The broader O-Closure principle extends the same idea beyond attention. Token-local maps must absorb zero, while support-coupled maps must assign zero mass to null sources. OFFN, ONorm, OInject, and OStandardize cover feed-forward branches, normalization, injected signals such as position information, and shared statistics. Residual addition and composition then give a conditional construction of an OTransformer.","experiments":"The empirical evidence is scoped. First, finite-precision tests verify the declared null-consistency properties: a null receiver returns zero, inserted null sources do not change previous outputs or weights, a zero carrier inserted into self-attention leaves old tokens unchanged and gives the new token zero output, and empty support returns finite zero outputs. Second, a frozen pretrained TabPFN v3 host is used to test calibrated near-identity compatibility, showing that the modified operators can be inserted under controlled conditions without immediately breaking the host. Third, a scoped OTransformer ablation shows that changing attention alone is insufficient; the whole computation path needs O-Closure. There are no large-scale LLM pretraining results, and the guarantees do not cover arbitrary tokenizers, routers, KV caches, or readouts.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough for most LLM researchers. Start with the failure cases for ordinary attention at the origin; they explain why masks and zero vectors solve different problems. Then read the four null-consistency conditions and the OAttention formula, especially the placement of presence on both receiver output and source mass. For implementation interest, scan the O-Closure constructions for OFFN, ONorm, OInject, and OStandardize. The most useful experimental point is the OTransformer ablation: null consistency is a whole-path property, not an attention-only patch.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"提出了一种基于 token 范数动态调节注意力权重的 OAttention，将零向量视为惰性 token，设计很 clean，值得关注其在稀疏或长上下文场景的潜力。","en":"Proposes OAttention, which dynamically adjusts attention weights based on token norms and treats zero vectors as inert tokens. Standard speculative decoding usually searches for a cheap token-level draft distribution. SSR instead uses the temporal structure of the reasoning trace: a partial CoT already induces an intermediate answer distribution, and that distribution tends to move closer to the final answer as reasoning progresses. This is also interesting for model researchers because it treats CoT as a gradually converging distributional process rather than only a human-readable rationale. The method is training-free and requires no auxiliary heads or architectural changes.","technical_approach":"SSR runs the same model at two effective reasoning budgets. Draft generation happens concurrently with continued CoT generation, so the draft cost can be hidden behind ongoing reasoning. Verification first accepts the longest left-to-right prefix that matches the final answer. For long-form outputs, early differences in phrasing or variable names can cause prefix verification to fail even when later spans still overlap. To recover this, SSR adds suffix decoding: it builds a suffix cache from the draft and proposes later matching spans after the first rejection. The paper also introduces an iterative variant that samples drafts at multiple points during CoT generation; earlier drafts are reused to bootstrap later drafts rather than being wasted.","experiments":"The provided content describes the method and claims but does not include the full experimental tables. Together these steps degrade reasoning, mathematics, coding, and long-context behavior enough to require a recovery, or healing, stage before deployment. The default recipe, quantization-aware training (QAT), re-fits the compressed, quantized model to hard labels; in our pipeline it converged slowly and collapsed past its peak. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T10:19:27Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20953.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是结构化压缩之后再做 4-bit 量化时的恢复问题。常见流程会先把大模型通过删头、删 FFN 神经元、删层或降维等方式压到更小架构，再量化到 4 bit。两步叠加会损伤数学、推理、代码和长上下文能力。直接做 QAT 收敛慢且容易过峰后崩掉；用量化感知蒸馏 QAD 时，如果 teacher 选压缩后的 bf16 checkpoint，又会被这个已经退化的中间模型限制住。","importance":"这篇论文对基座研究的价值主要在部署前恢复和压缩训练 recipe，而不是预训练算法。关键启发是：在 compound compression 场景里，量化不是一个单独的后处理步骤，它可以作为第二次接受强 teacher 监督的阶段。teacher 选择比 fake quant 技巧本身更重要。对于需要发布小参数、低 bit 模型的团队，这个结论会影响压缩链路设计：不要默认把压缩后 bf16 模型当作量化 teacher，原始未压缩模型的 logits 可能提供更高上限。","technical_approach":"Quantization-Aware Healing 使用已经完成 post-training 的原始未压缩模型作为 teacher，直接蒸馏结构化压缩且 4-bit fake-quantized 的 student。流程里，原始高精度模型 M_HP 先经过结构化压缩算子 S 得到更小架构 M_C；M_C 再通过 KL 蒸馏从 M_HP 恢复为 bf16 checkpoint M_BF16；随后量化算子 Q 把 M_BF16 转成 MXFP4。标准 QAD 会用 M_BF16 监督 4-bit student，但 M_BF16 本身只是压缩后恢复模型，并非独立训练出来的强 teacher。QAH 改为用 M_HP 的输出分布监督 4-bit student。由于 KL 蒸馏只依赖输出分布，teacher 和 student 不需要同构。这个改动简单，但直接避开了“用弱中间 checkpoint 固定学生上限”的问题。","experiments":"实验链路是 GPT-OSS 120B 压缩到 60B，再量化为 MXFP4。论文报告 QAH student 在 9 个 benchmark 中有 7 个达到或超过自己的 bf16 source，并且在 LiveCodeBench 上达到原始 120B 模型水平。资源上，student 参数量是 teacher 的一半，权重内存约低 4 倍。与匹配的 QAT baseline 相比，QAH 收敛约快 7 倍，并且不依赖手工调 early stopping；QAT 在过峰后会出现 collapse，部署上更难控。论文还记录了不同分布式训练 backend 之间存在较大且可复现的质量差异，这是实际复现和上线时需要注意的工程变量。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20 到 30 分钟。先看 introduction 里对 QAT、QAD、QAH 三者适用场景的区分，重点是为什么结构化压缩之后不存在一个理想的同架构 full-precision teacher。然后看方法部分的三阶段模型定义：M_HP、M_BF16、MXFP4 student，以及 KL 蒸馏目标如何跨架构工作。实验部分重点看 120B→60B→MXFP4 的 benchmark 表、QAT 训练曲线、QAH 收敛速度和 early stopping 稳定性。第 6 节的部署经验值得快速读，尤其是分布式 backend 造成质量差异这一点。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses recovery after compound compression: structural compression followed by 4-bit quantization. Structural compression changes the architecture and reduces parameter count; quantization then lowers precision. Together they hurt reasoning, math, coding, and long-context behavior. Standard QAT is slow and fragile in this setting. QAD is safer for quantization-only pipelines, but after structural compression its natural teacher is the recovered bf16 compressed model, which is already a degraded approximation of the original model.","importance":"The useful lesson is about the compression and deployment recipe. In compound compression, the quantization stage should not be treated only as a lossy conversion step. It can be another distillation stage, and the choice of teacher matters more than the presence of fake quantization alone. For teams building smaller low-bit variants of large models, the paper argues against using the compressed bf16 checkpoint as the default teacher for quantization recovery. The original uncompressed model can provide a higher-quality training signal even when teacher and student architectures differ.","technical_approach":"Quantization-Aware Healing trains the compressed 4-bit student with fake quantization while distilling directly from the original uncompressed model. The pipeline starts with a high-precision model M_HP that has already gone through post-training. A structural operator S produces a smaller architecture M_C. This compressed model is recovered in bf16 by KL distillation from M_HP, giving M_BF16. A quantization operator then produces an MXFP4 student. Standard QAD would use M_BF16 as the teacher, but that teacher has already absorbed compression loss. QAH instead uses the output distribution of M_HP as the target for the 4-bit student. Because the loss is distribution-level KL, the teacher and student do not need to share an architecture.","experiments":"The reported pipeline compresses GPT-OSS 120B to 60B and then quantizes it to MXFP4. The QAH student matches or exceeds its bf16 source on 7 of 9 benchmarks and reaches the original 120B model on LiveCodeBench. It uses half the teacher’s parameters and roughly 4x less weight memory. We frame calibration-data selection for quantization as a target-dependent uncertainty-preservation problem. Different deployments emphasize different regions of the input distribution, yet prior work mainly optimizes accuracy-oriented compression metrics or adjusts scores after quantization. We formalize this ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T12:07:49Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21019.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是量化后精度掉多少，而是量化后模型的“不确定性行为”还能不能保持和全精度模型一致。更具体地说，作者把 calibration data selection 重新定义为一个目标相关的问题：如果部署场景关心的是整体概率分布、置信度、top-2 margin、重排序分数，那么应优化 distributional preservation；如果关心的是 answerability、abstention、低 margin 决策边界是否翻转，那么应优化 boundary preservation。论文要解决的核心问题，是在不改量化器本身的前提下，如何选择一小批校准数据，让量化模型尽量保留全精度模型在这些不确定性信号上的行为，而不是只保留 top-1 正确率。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在预训练本身，而在“模型压缩后哪些行为会被破坏、哪些信号值得保真”这件事上给出了更细的视角。很多训练和后训练工作默认 logits、confidence、margin 这些量在部署压缩后仍可用，但这篇文章说明：即便 top-1 不变，置信度结构也可能明显漂移，进而影响 abstention、reranking、verification、ensemble 等依赖分数的系统。它的贡献量级更像一个部署侧方法学补丁，而不是范式变化：一是把 uncertainty preservation 明确成量化校准目标；二是给出 target-dependent 的形式化风险；三是用 mixture-mismatch 论证说明不存在对所有目标都合适的单一 calibration recipe。对做 pretrain/post-train 接口的人，这个结论有现实意义：训练时学到的概率结构，未必能在低比特部署中自然保留下来。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者提出 DPQ（Doubt-Preserving Quantization），它不是新的量化算法，而是一族 pre-quantization calibration data recipe。核心做法是先用全精度模型在候选样本上打分，再按部署目标挑选“脆弱样本”。两类信号最关键：一类是 high-doubt 样本，比如高熵、低置信度、小 top-2 margin，这些样本对分布扰动更敏感；另一类是 answerability boundary 附近的样本，尤其适合 SQuAD2 这类 answer/no-answer 决策。然后把这些 target-aware 样本与 generic anchors 混合，送入原封不动的量化器，例如 GPTQ、AWQ 等。论文的理论部分不复杂，但有一个有用的判断：如果目标分布更重视边界附近样本，而 calibration mixture 主要由普通 anchor 组成，那么最优校准集不会与面向 broad MCQA 的最优校准集一致；反过来也一样。这就是作者所谓 mixture-mismatch。实验里 DPQ 有多种 recipe，例如按 boundary signal 和 generic anchor 的比例配比，像 s128-r75 这类配置对应固定样本数和混合比。文章还区分了两类 preservation risk：一类是分布层面的风险，关注量化后概率分布与 FP 分布的偏离；另一类是边界层面的风险，关注低 margin 区域的决策翻转，并可用权重函数强调 answerability 或 selective prediction 场景。这个 framing 比单纯看 ECE 或 accuracy 更贴近实际系统。","experiments":"实验覆盖 8 个语言模型、9 个 NLP benchmark、22 种比较方法或配置，评测维度明显比一般量化论文更偏“行为保真”。指标除了常见的 ECE、adaptive ECE、NLL、Brier score，还包括和全精度行为直接对齐的 FP agreement、JSD(pQ, p0)、confidence shift、top-two margin drift、margin correlation，以及 SQuAD2 上的 answerability boundary 指标。主要结论有三点。第一，最优 calibration recipe 明显依赖目标：SQuAD2 这类边界敏感任务上，高 boundary mixture 的 DPQ-s128-r75 表现最好；而 broad MCQA 更偏好较温和的 recipe，如 DPQ-r50、只看 confidence 或只看 entropy 的选择策略。第二，单看 accuracy 往往掩盖了不确定性漂移；有些配置 top-1 基本不变，但 confidence/margin 指标明显恶化。第三，信号有一定可迁移性，但量化器相关性仍然存在：作者做了 post-hoc calibration 和 AWQ 分析，发现 preservation signal 不是完全绑定某一个量化器，但 score-based metric 的最优 recipe 仍带有 quantizer-specific 特征。摘要里没有给出完整逐表数字，全文重点也不是追求某个统一平均提升，而是证明“目标依赖”这个现象在多模型多任务上稳定存在。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不需要逐式推导。建议先看 Introduction 和 Problem Statement，抓住作者为什么把 calibration data selection 从 accuracy 导向改成 uncertainty-preservation 导向。然后重点看定义的两类风险：distributional risk 和 boundary risk，它们对应两种部署目标。接着看 mixture-mismatch 的论证，不必纠结形式证明，关键是理解为什么不存在一个对所有任务都稳妥的固定 calibration mixture。实验部分优先看 SQuAD2 与 MCQA 的对比表，以及各类 FP-behavior 指标，那里最能体现“accuracy 不变但 uncertainty 变了”。如果时间有限，30 到 40 分钟足够抓住主线。值得深挖的点有两个：一是这种 target-aware calibration 是否能推广到更大模型和更极端低比特；二是能否把 preservation target 直接并入量化目标，而不是只在数据选择层面做文章。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about preserving top-1 accuracy under quantization. It asks a narrower and more deployment-relevant question: can a quantized LM preserve the uncertainty behavior of its full-precision counterpart? The authors cast calibration-data selection as a target-dependent uncertainty-preservation problem. If the downstream use cares about probability distributions, confidence, margins, reranking, or ensembling, the objective is distributional preservation. If it cares about abstention, answerability, or low-margin decision boundaries, the objective is boundary preservation. The core problem is how to choose a small calibration set, without changing the quantizer itself, so that the quantized model stays behaviorally close to the FP model in these uncertainty-sensitive regimes.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model researchers is indirect but real. Many training and post-training pipelines assume that logits, confidence, and margins remain usable after low-bit deployment. This paper shows that this assumption is weak: the top prediction can stay unchanged while confidence structure shifts enough to affect abstention, verification, reranking, and selective prediction. The contribution is methodological rather than paradigm-level. It formalizes uncertainty preservation as a first-class quantization objective, separates target distributions into distributional vs boundary-sensitive cases, and argues that no single calibration recipe should be expected to work across all targets. For people working on the pretrain-to-deployment interface, the message is simple: probability structure learned during training is not automatically preserved by compression.","technical_approach":"$11","experiments":"$12","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Start with the introduction and problem formulation to understand why the authors move from accuracy-oriented calibration to uncertainty-preserving calibration. Focus on the two preservation risks, because they map cleanly to two deployment regimes. Then read the mixture-mismatch argument; the exact proof matters less than the practical implication that one fixed calibration mixture is not universally appropriate. In the experiments, prioritize the SQuAD2 vs MCQA comparisons and the FP-behavior metrics. That is where the paper makes its case most clearly: unchanged accuracy does not imply preserved uncertainty. A 30-40 minute read is enough to extract the main idea. If you want to go deeper, the natural follow-up questions are whether this extends to larger models and lower-bit settings, and whether uncertainty preservation can be built into the quantization objective itself rather than handled only through calibration-data selection.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"量化论文里少见地把 uncertainty 当主目标，不再只看 perplexity/accuracy；先读 mixture-mismatch 论证和 calibration set 构造，若指标定义站得住，这会影响你做拒答与 verifier 的部署 recipe。","en":"Few quantization papers treat uncertainty as the primary objective instead of just perplexity or accuracy. This makes ordinary decoding memory-efficient, but hurts speculative decoding. To verify a batch of draft tokens and then roll back the rejected ones, today's systems snapshot the full recurrent state at every draft position for GDN layers, and those snapshots cannot be shared across branches of a draft tree, so a wid","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T10:31:15Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20961.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.DC","cs.LG","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"TreeWY 解决的是混合架构模型在 speculative decoding 中的 Gated DeltaNet 回滚问题。GDN 层不像 softmax attention 那样保留逐 token KV cache，而是把前缀压到固定大小的 recurrent state 里。普通解码时这很省内存，但验证 draft token 或 draft tree 时，系统需要回滚到被接受节点的状态。现有做法通常为每个 draft 位置保存完整 recurrent state，树状 draft 又无法共享这些快照，导致验证阶段的瞬时显存随 draft 节点数快速增长。","importance":"这篇对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对混合 attention/linear-attention 架构的落地很关键。GDN、Mamba 类层被引入大模型，一个主要动机是降低长上下文 KV cache 成本；如果 speculative decoding 反而要求大量 state snapshot，服务端吞吐优势会被抵消。TreeWY 说明这类 recurrent linear-attention 层需要配套的验证算法，而不能直接套用 Transformer 的 KV rollback 机制。对基座研究的启发是：架构设计不能只看训练 loss 和单步解码内存，也要看 speculative verification、tree decoding、rollback 等 serving 路径是否有代数结构可用。","technical_approach":"核心方法是把 Gated DeltaNet 的 gated delta recurrence 改写成树结构上的 WY/UT 形式。GDN 的状态转移是标量 decay 乘以 rank-1 correction：T_t = alpha_t(I - beta_t k_t k_t^T)。这个转移不是简单可交换的标量门控，所以不能像 Mamba2 的 STree 那样用累积和处理整棵树。TreeWY 利用严格祖先关系构造树状闭式表达，通过一次 triangular solve 得到所有 draft 节点的输出。验证阶段不再为每个节点保存完整 recurrent state；提交时只重建最终被接受路径对应的一个状态，并用一个较小的 pseudo-value matrix 替代 per-node snapshot。它和 ReplaySSM 的差别主要在状态物化时机和 tree 支持：ReplaySSM 也在窗口内解三角系统，但以 chain-only 的延迟物化为主；TreeWY 面向 draft tree，把分支共享纳入同一个代数形式。","experiments":"全文给出的关键数字主要是内存账。作者以两个 Qwen3.5 混合模型为例：35B-A3B 有 30 个 GDN 层、10 个 softmax 层、32 个 value heads；397B-A17B 有 45 个 GDN 层、15 个 softmax 层、64 个 value heads；两者都是 3:1 GDN-to-softmax，GDN 中 d_k = d_v = 128，softmax head dim 为 256，2 个 KV heads。普通解码时 softmax KV cache 约为 20 KiB/token 和 30 KiB/token，在 32K context 下约为 0.625 GiB/seq 和 0.94 GiB/seq；GDN committed state 固定为约 30 MiB/seq 和 90 MiB/seq。speculative verification 中，如果 k=3 且采用 full-state snapshot，需要 4 份 GDN state，即约 120 MiB/seq 和 360 MiB/seq；draft tree 则需要 N+1 份，且分支无法共享。文中声称实现位于 vLLM，并在附录与 ReplaySSM 比较，但给定内容没有包含完整吞吐、延迟或端到端接受率结果，阅读时需要核对实验章节和附录 D。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25 分钟。先看 Background 和 Memory problem，确认 GDN 层为什么省普通解码显存却放大 speculative verification 的回滚成本。然后看 Section 3 的 tree-structured WY/UT 推导，重点是从 chain WY 到 tree strict-ancestor 形式的推广，以及 triangular solve 如何同时覆盖所有 draft 节点。最后看实现章节和 Appendix D，检查它在 vLLM 中如何接入 scheduler、state commit、tree drafter，以及和 ReplaySSM 的实际差异。若只关心预训练架构选型，重点看内存账和适用条件；若关心 serving，需要细读 kernel、数值稳定性和 tree 宽度下的吞吐结果。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"TreeWY addresses a serving bottleneck introduced by Gated DeltaNet hybrid models during speculative decoding. GDN layers keep a fixed-size recurrent state instead of a token-growing KV cache. That is attractive for normal decoding, but verification of draft chains or draft trees requires rollback to the accepted node. Current systems usually snapshot the full recurrent state at every draft position, and those snapshots cannot be shared across branches, making wide speculative trees memory-expensive.","importance":"The work is not a pretraining method, but it matters for the practical viability of hybrid attention and linear-attention LMs. GDN-style layers are often motivated by lower KV-cache growth at long context. If speculative decoding requires full recurrent-state snapshots, the serving advantage can disappear during verification. The useful lesson for base-model research is that architecture choices should be evaluated together with the decoding algorithms they enable or block. A recurrent layer needs algebraic structure not only for training and single-token decoding, but also for rollback, tree verification, and commit.","technical_approach":"The method rewrites the Gated DeltaNet recurrence into a tree-structured WY/UT form. A GDN transition is a scalar decay times a rank-1 correction, T_t = alpha_t(I - beta_t k_t k_t^T). Unlike Mamba2-style scalar transitions, these matrices do not commute, so a simple cumulative-sum trick over gates is not sufficient. TreeWY constructs a closed form over strict ancestor relationships in the draft tree and computes the outputs for all draft nodes with a triangular solve. It avoids storing a full recurrent-state snapshot per node. On commit, it reconstructs only the accepted state and uses a compact pseudo-value matrix as the transient object. Compared with ReplaySSM, the overlap is the triangular solve inside the verification window; the difference is that TreeWY directly targets tree verification rather than mainly chain-only deferred materialization.","experiments":"The provided content gives a detailed memory analysis rather than full throughput numbers. The examples are two Qwen3.5 hybrids: 35B-A3B with 30 GDN layers, 10 softmax layers, and 32 value heads; and 397B-A17B with 45 GDN layers, 15 softmax layers, and 64 value heads. Both use a 3:1 GDN-to-softmax ratio, d_k = d_v = 128 for GDN, softmax head dimension 256, and 2 KV heads. In normal decoding, the softmax KV cache costs about 20 KiB/token and 30 KiB/token, or 0.625 GiB/sequence and 0.94 GiB/sequence at 32K context. The committed GDN state is fixed at about 30 MiB/sequence and 90 MiB/sequence. With full-state snapshotting and k=3 draft tokens, verification needs four GDN states, about 120 MiB/sequence and 360 MiB/sequence; a tree with N nodes needs N+1 states and cannot share snapshots across branches. The text says the method is implemented in vLLM and compared with ReplaySSM in Appendix D, but the supplied content does not include the final latency, throughput, or end-to-end acceptance-rate results.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 minutes. Read the background and memory accounting first; they explain why GDN is memory-efficient for normal decoding but problematic for speculative rollback. Then read the Section 3 derivation, focusing on how the chain WY transform is extended to a tree through strict-ancestor structure and why a triangular solve is enough to verify all draft nodes. Finally inspect the implementation section and Appendix D for scheduler integration, accepted-state reconstruction, and comparisons with ReplaySSM. For architecture researchers, the memory model and applicability conditions are the most useful parts. For serving work, kernel details, numerical behavior, and tree-width scaling are the key checks.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看推导和内存模型；若团队押注 DeltaNet/线性注意力混合模型，TreeWY 值得做一次内部复现。","en":"Read the derivation and memory model; if your stack bets on DeltaNet-like hybrids, TreeWY is worth an internal reproduction."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Thomson Reuters","Affiliation:Thomson Reuters"],"authors":["Sneha Murthy Ghantasala"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["speculative-decoding","linear-attention","gated-deltanet","serving","memory-optimization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20961","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.20961","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.20743","title":"Is Multimodal Speculative Decoding Ready for Diffusion-Based Parallel Drafting? charac","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T18:35:12Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20427.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长上下文 key/value block 包括四部分：当前 block 的因果三角、自身前面两个局部 block、全局第一个 block、以及按 dyadic 距离回看的历史 block，也就是 j-2^r 这类前驱块，从 4 blocks 的偏移开始。形式上这是 local + global-first + dyadic history 的组合。\n\n作者明确承认这和早期的 LogSparse、LongNet 一脉相承，创新点不在路由图本身，而在 pretrained-model retrofit、matched-control 训练比较、以及 Blackwell GPU 上的 correctness-gated 系统测量。所谓 correctness-gated，意思是他们不只跑快路径 benchmark，而是逐项验证 partial blocks、backward gradients、dense-path equivalence、future-token isolation、顺序反转 benchmark 等，确保稀疏实现没有偷偷破坏因果性或数值正确性。\n\n复杂度上，固定 block width 时，每个转换层的 selected interactions 是 O(n log n)，图通信深度是 O(log n)。这里的“通信深度”可以理解为信息从远处 token 传到当前位置需要跨多少层稀疏边，dyadic predecessor 让这个深度从线性降到对数级。和纯 local sliding window 相比，这是 BF1 的结构优势；和随机非局部图相比，它是确定性的、可复现的，也更容易做 kernel 优化。\n\n论文还做了一个很重要的设计选择：不是把所有层都改成 BF1，而是做 selected eight-layer retrofit，并把这个真实改造的 whole-model TTFT 与“全层都换掉”的 timing-only ceiling 分开报告。这个做法比较诚实，也更接近生产系统，因为很多时候不是每一层都适合稀疏化，尤其在已有预训练模型上直接替换全部注意力层风险很高。","experiments":"$13","reading_guide":"泛读时建议按“算子定义—系统测量—训练对照”三步走。先看 This is highly relevant for production-deployed LLMs, allowing for reversible fine-tuning without invalidating existing certification frameworks, offering a low-cost path for continual learning and model editing.","technical_approach":"Proposes In-Cell Learning (CellFill). The core idea is to freeze the integer codes and scaling factors of a 4-bit quantized model and write full-precision residuals strictly within the quantization decision cell of each weight (the interval discarded during quantization). Upon re-quantization, the model reproduces the original released artifact bit-for-bit. It applies alternating closed-form left orthogonal rotation and manifold-optimized right orthogonal rotation to the retained weights. 策略的影响，再补读案例分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a concrete systems problem: how to benchmark LLM serving under agentic workloads in a reproducible way. The difficulty is that agent execution graphs are induced by model outputs. Small decoding differences can change whether the agent calls a tool, branches, reflects, or stops, which then changes the number of LLM calls, prompt lengths, output lengths, and dependency structure. The paper shows this is not a minor effect: running the same request twice with mini-SWE-agent and gpt-oss-120b yields about 70% variation in the number of LLM calls on average. That makes conventional serving benchmarks, which assume independent requests or simple multi-turn chats, a poor proxy for real agent traffic.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for foundation-model researchers because it sharpens the interface between model capability and deployment cost. As models become better at tool use, long-context reasoning, and multi-step planning, serving behavior is shaped less by single-call throughput and more by execution-graph structure, cache reuse, and concurrency patterns. judgme","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T10:01:58Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20938.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决的是版本化 AI evaluator 的“判决变化可归因”问题。一个 evaluator 从旧版本到新版本，最终标签可能仍然正确，但原因可能错了；也可能标签发生变化，却无法说明变化来自证据、规则，还是规则适用权限。普通 accuracy、pairwise agreement、自由文本 rationale 都无法检测这种错误。问题在于：当 evaluator 的输出用于放行 agent 行为、分配人工审核、或回流为训练奖励时，错误的理由会污染后续决策和训练信号。","importance":"对预训练和后训练的影响主要在 evaluator 作为训练信号入口这一层。当前很多 RLHF、RLAIF、verifier、judge model、agent gating 系统只记录最终偏好或通过/拒绝标签，但没有验证标签变化是否由声明的证据或规则变化导致。这项工作给出一个可执行的归因单元：不是要求模型写一段解释，而是要求列出哪些源替换足以复现新判决。贡献量级不在模型能力提升，而在评测协议和反馈信号质量控制。它提醒训练系统不要只看 judge 的最终分数，还要检查 judge 在规则版本、证据版本、权限边界变化时是否保持因果一致。对于 reward hacking、verifier drift、数据回流污染，这是一类更细的检测工具。","technical_approach":"方法把 evaluator 的局部状态拆成三类有类型的来源：grounds 表示事实和证据，norms 表示决策规则，authority 表示规则是否适用于当前 case。旧状态和新状态之间，每一类来源都可以取旧版或新版，因此形成 2^3 个混合状态，称为 judgment cube。对每个混合状态执行可信 adjudicator，得到对应判决。若某个来源替换集合足以让旧状态复现新判决，并且没有更小的子集也能做到，则它是一个 inclusion-minimal sufficient replacement。所有这样的最小替换集合组成 judgment receipt。核心区分很重要：模型生成的 receipt 只是预测，只有把 counterfactual hybrid 真正送入可信 adjudicator 执行后，receipt 才能被认证。工作还引入 paired consistency。每个控制变换都带有已知输出关系，类似软件测试里的 metamorphic relation。若一对样本违反关系，就能确定至少一个预测错误，从而在没有变换后 gold label 的情况下得到错误率下界。这个设计比普通 robustness accuracy 更强，因为它检查的是变化关系，而不是孤立标签。","experiments":"实验资产是 ReasonBench，包含 45 条经审计的组织政策条款和 800 个独立生成的逻辑世界。每个样本都有可执行的旧状态、新状态、八个 hybrid 状态，以及精确的 receipt family。source unit 在 prompt rendering 前已经拆分，paired control 的标签由构造保证。关键结果不是单纯准确率，而是准确率和理由一致性的分离。作者预先冻结过一个假设：用 full-cube supervision 监督八个 cell 会优于直接监督 receipt。结果相反，full-cube supervision 没赢。原因是八个 cell 的联合预测比 receipt 本身更难，密集监督没有自动转化为更好的归因。更有信息量的失败包括：模型在测试准确率上接近 ceiling，但在 source reordering 下的一致性只有约 50%； revised verdict 在 composition shift 下相对稳定，但 receipt 不稳定。这说明 evaluator 可以学会给出对的最终判决，却无法稳定说明是哪类来源变化导致了判决。","reading_guide":"建议精读形式化定义和 ReasonBench 构造，而不是只看总体分数。重点看三处：第一，grounds、norms、authority 的类型化拆分，以及 judgment cube 和 judgment receipt 的定义，这是核心抽象；第二，paired consistency 和 Proposition 5，它说明为什么不需要变换后 gold label 也能得到错误下界；第三，实验里的 negative result，尤其是 full-cube supervision 失败、source reordering consistency 约 50%、verdict 与 receipt 在 composition shift 下表现分离。这些结果对训练 judge/verifier 很有用：要把最终标签正确性和原因归因正确性拆开评估。若用于 LLM 训练系统，可以进一步思考如何把 receipt certification 接入 reward model 更新、policy patching、或数据回流过滤。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work targets attribution for versioned AI evaluators. An evaluator may keep the correct final label while changing for the wrong reason, or it may change its verdict without making clear whether the change came from evidence, rules, or rule applicability. Final-label accuracy, agreement metrics, and free-form rationales do not expose this failure. The risk is practical: these evaluators often gate agent actions, route cases to human review, and feed labels back into training as reward or supervision.","importance":"The main relevance to foundation-model training is signal quality. Modern RLHF, RLAIF, verifier training, judge models, and agent control loops often treat evaluator outputs as scalar labels. This work argues that scalar correctness is too weak when the evaluator itself is versioned. A reward or verifier signal should not only be right; its change should be traceable to a declared change in evidence, rule, or authority. The contribution is an evaluation and certification protocol rather than a new model. It gives a concrete way to detect evaluator drift, inertia, and misattribution before those errors are recycled into training data or automated decisions.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"The benchmark, ReasonBench, contains 45 audited organizational policy clauses and 800 independently generated logical worlds. Each item has executable old and revised states, all eight hybrid states, and the exact receipt family. Source units are split before prompt rendering, and paired controls are labeled by construction. The most useful empirical result is the gap between verdict accuracy and reason stability. A pre-registered hypothesis that full-cube supervision would help did not hold: supervising all eight cells is a harder target than predicting the receipt, and denser supervision did not translate into better attribution. The failures are more informative than the headline score. Models can reach near-ceiling test accuracy while dropping to roughly 50% consistency under source reordering. Revised verdicts remain more stable under composition shifts than receipts do. The result shows that correct judgments and correct accounts of judgment change are separable capabilities.","reading_guide":"Read the formal sections carefully. The important pieces are the typed source decomposition, the judgment cube, and the definition of a complete minimal receipt. Then focus on the paired-consistency construction and Proposition 5, since this is the part that turns metamorphic relations into a label-efficient diagnostic. The experimental section is worth reading for the negative result: full-cube supervision failing, source-reordering consistency near 50%, and the split between stable verdicts and unstable receipts. For training-system design, the useful question is how receipt certification could be attached to verifier updates, reward-model audits, policy-version changes, or filtering of judge-generated training data.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"对用 judge 产 RL/SFT 信号的人有用；重点看 receipt 定义和黑盒认证成本，别只看高准确率。","en":"Useful if judges feed RL or SFT data; read the receipt definition and black-box certification cost, not just the high accuracy numbers."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Alibaba Group","Cheung Kong Graduate School of Business","Affiliation:Alibaba Group","Affiliation:Cheung Kong Graduate School of Business"],"authors":["Ye Chen","Weining Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["evaluator","reasoning-benchmark","judge-reliability","counterfactual-evaluation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20938","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.20938","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.21360","title":"OmniAssistBench: Assistant-style Interaction Benchmark for Omni-LLMs","abstract":"Recent omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) show great potential as real-time video assistants, which continuously perceive environments and guide users to achieve specific goals. of","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T17:59:52Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21360.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"评测“实时视频助手型”Omni-LLM omni-modal LLMs as real-time video assistants is hard because interaction paths diverge: the assistant’s response changes the user’s next action, which changes subsequent video inputs. Static offline QA benchmarks cannot capture this closed-loop dynamic. The paper addresses how to build a reproducible, scorable benchmark that still reflects assistant-style multi-turn video interactions.","importance":"Primarily an evaluation contribution, with indirect but useful feedback to pretraining: it operationalizes assistant-style bottlenecks (visual prompt following, long-term memory under context limits, delayed response, cross-turn goal retention). These failure modes often trace back to pretraining/mid-training data formats and objectives, not only post-training. If you build omni-modal or long-context pretraining pipelines, the benchmark’s taxonomy can guide what data and objectives are missing.","technical_approach":"OmniAssistBench fixes interaction-path divergence by introducing explicit video-derived priors.\n- Reverse-engineer Internet videos: select suitable source videos, infer user goals and a standard procedural path (priors), then segment videos into multi-turn clips to simulate step-by-step assistant interactions.\n- During evaluation, provide the priors to the model and require guidance along a single route, reducing dependence on the model’s internal world knowledge and avoiding the need to dynamically generate new video branches.\n- Two-tier framework: Basic tier for foundational interactive perception/instruction-following (e.g., gestures, social relations); Advanced tier for goal-oriented tasks using everyday plot templates.\n- Additionally, film three long cases (~15 turns each) to test combined abilities; user instructions are embedded as audio; open-ended scoring penalizes redundancy and hallucinations.","experiments":"Reported headline results: Gemini-3-Pro scores 66.4/100; open-source Qwen3-Omni-Instruct scores 51.2/100. More detailed verification requires the full paper’s per-skill breakdown, scoring rubric, annotator agreement, and baseline coverage.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 minutes, benchmark-oriented):\n- Data construction: how priors are defined and presented; this determines whether the benchmark measures assistant reasoning or script regurgitation.\n- Scoring protocol: how open-ended answers are graded, how hallucination/redundancy penalties work, and inter-rater reliability.\n- Task taxonomy and examples: map each failure mode to likely training gaps (gesture-following data, temporal “wait” behavior, long-context training, cross-turn goal tracking).\n- The three long cases: useful for debugging context budgeting, goal persistence, and delayed-response behavior.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"针对Omni-LLM交互能力的评测，通过逆向工程视频构建交互路径的思路比较巧妙，值得关注其具体的评测指标设计。","en":"A benchmark for Omni-LLM interactive capabilities. However, most existing benchmarks are limited to the document level and focus mainly on high-resource languages, leaving many fine-grained challenges insufficiently evaluated. To address this gap, we present MGAL, the first multilingual, granularity- and position-aware long-context benchmark. MGAL is constructed from United Nations (UN) reports spanning 8K to 128K tokens across the six official UN languages. It covers ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T08:19:00Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20853.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“再做一个长上下文榜单”，而是把长上下文能力拆开测清楚。现有 benchmark 多半停留在文档级任务，语言覆盖偏高资源语种，也很少控制证据在长文中的位置，因此很难判断模型到底是不会跨粒度理解，还是只是被位置偏置、表面词重叠、评分方式等因素干扰。MGAL 试图把这几个变量显式分离：同一套长文材料上，同时考察词、句、段、文档四个粒度，并按 begin/middle/end 控制证据位置，再扩展到联合国六种官方语言。它要回答的是：长上下文模型在不同语言、不同粒度、不同证据位置下，失效模式到底长什么样。","importance":"对基座研究的价值主要在评测设计，而不是提出新模型。它补上了两个长期缺口。第一，长上下文评测过去偏“粗粒度”，容易把很多局部检索、句法衔接、段落功能理解的问题淹没在文档级分数里；MGAL 把能力分解后，更适合诊断训练目标、位置编码、长序列注意力和数据配比带来的具体短板。第二，它把多语言和位置控制做到了同一框架里，这对分析长上下文 scaling 是否只在英语成立、以及低资源语言为何掉队，很有帮助。对做预训练的人来说，这类 benchmark 的意义不在 leaderboard，而在于它能把“模型为什么错”暴露得更具体：是 recency bias、option-order bias、局部语义拥挤，还是生成时的 input anchoring 不足。","technical_approach":"MGAL 的核心做法是用联合国数字图书馆中的长篇报告构造一个位置对齐、跨语言可比的长上下文测试集。数据覆盖阿拉伯语、中文、英语、法语、俄语、西班牙语，输入长度从 8K 到 128K token。任务设计上分成 closed-ended 和 open-ended 两类，并覆盖四层语言粒度：词级、句级、段级、文档级。文中提到的任务包括 word-level QA、sentence-level cloze、paragraph filling、document summarization、translation 等，共 7 个任务；每种语言每个任务有 420 个 query-response 对。关键不只是任务种类，而是构造时同时控制了证据位置：在文档级和段落级都标注 begin/middle/end，使得同一模型在不同位置上的退化可以被直接比较。\n\n数据来源选 UN 报告有两个原因。一个是天然多语平行和主题一致，便于做跨语言对照；另一个是文档结构较规范，适合定义词、句、段、文档四层 coherent unit。作者强调 same-position sentences 在六种语言中语义对齐，这让跨语言比较不只是“题型类似”，而是材料位置和语义都尽量一致。质量控制上，所有 query-response pair 都经过人工审查并删除有缺陷样本，明显是偏小而精的 benchmark 路线。\n\n评分方面，closed-ended 任务主要用 Accuracy，开放生成任务用 ROUGE-L 等 reference-based 指标；对于 paragraph filling 这类仅靠 n-gram overlap 很难评准的任务，又引入了 LLM-as-a-judge。这个设计比较务实：如果只用自动指标，会低估语义正确但表述不同的答案；如果全靠 judge，又会把 benchmark 的可复现性变差。MGAL 采取的是两者并用。\n\n从方法贡献看，它不是提出新的评测算法，而是把三个维度系统化了：multilingual、granularity-aware、position-aware。和 LongBench、LV-Eval、M4LE、ONERULER 这类已有工作相比，区别不在规模更大，而在控制变量更细，能支持更明确的误差归因。","experiments":"实验部分的重点不是谁第一，而是错误模式。作者评测了 12 个 long-context LLM。摘要和正文给出的主要结论有四类。\n\n第一，粒度越粗，性能越差。模型在词级任务上普遍表现较好，但从句子、段落到文档，能力明显下降。这说明很多模型的“长上下文能力”更接近局部匹配或局部检索，而不是稳定的跨段组织与全篇整合。\n\n第二，多语言差异在低资源语言上被放大。大开源模型和闭源模型在高资源语言上的差距没有想象中大，但到了低资源语言，闭源模型优势更稳定，小型开源模型掉得更明显。这个现象对预训练很关键，因为它暗示长上下文能力并不是单独由架构决定，语料覆盖和跨语言表示质量仍然是主导因素。\n\n第三，位置敏感性依然明显。sentence-cloze 中，空缺出现在文本前部时错误率最高，越靠后越好，和 recency-biased attention 的已有观察一致。作者还发现明显的 early-option bias：模型更容易选前面的选项，如 A/B，而不是逐项核对证据。这说明一部分错误不是“不会读长文”，而是推理过程被答题格式诱导。\n\n第四，作者提出了两个更细的失效现象。其一是 local semantic crowding：相邻句子共享大量实体、年份、连接词时，模型容易根据表面重叠选错句子，忽略句子在段落中的功能角色，比如背景、解释、结果。其二是 fluency-consistency gap：生成结果往往流畅、风格也对，但和输入锚定不够，容易出现 factual drift 或无依据补写。这个观察对长上下文生成很重要，因为很多模型在 summarization/filling 上看起来“像对了”，但其实证据绑定很弱。\n\n从数据规模上，benchmark 覆盖 6 种语言、7 个任务、4 个粒度、上下文长度 8K–128K，每语言每任务 420 个样本。虽然正文截断处没有完整表格数字，但这些设置已经足够支撑细粒度切片分析。若做复现实验，最值得关注的是不同模型在 begin/middle/end 和不同 granularity 上的相对斜率，而不是单一总分。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不需要逐段精读。建议先看 Introduction 和任务定义表，确认它到底比 LongBench 一类工作多控制了哪些变量。然后重点看 benchmark construction：数据来源、跨语言位置对齐、人工审核流程，这决定了评测结论是否可信。实验部分优先看按 granularity 和 position 分层的主结果图，而不是总榜单；真正有信息量的是性能如何随词→句→段→文档退化，以及 begin/middle/end 的差异曲线。再看作者对 local semantic crowding 和 fluency-consistency gap 的案例分析，这两部分最接近“为什么模型会错”。如果时间有限，30–40 分钟足够抓住主要信息。若你在做长上下文预训练或位置编码，值得把相关图表和错误案例细看一遍，因为它们能直接转化成训练诊断假设。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not trying to add another long-context leaderboard. Its actual target is diagnosis. Existing benchmarks mostly evaluate document-level tasks, focus on high-resource languages, and rarely control where the supporting evidence appears in the context. That makes it hard to tell whether a model fails because it cannot reason across discourse units, because it is position-biased, or because the task setup hides the failure mode. MGAL isolates these factors explicitly: it evaluates word-, sentence-, paragraph-, and document-level understanding, stratifies evidence by begin/middle/end positions, and does so across the six official UN languages. The central question is: what exactly breaks in long-context LLMs when granularity, language, and evidence position are varied in a controlled way?","importance":"Its value is in benchmark design rather than model innovation. Two gaps are addressed. First, long-context evaluation has been too coarse. Document-level scores often hide failures in local retrieval, sentence function discrimination, paragraph coherence, and evidence anchoring. By decomposing performance by granularity, MGAL is more useful for diagnosing effects of pretraining data mix, positional schemes, long-sequence attention, and instruction tuning. Second, it puts multilinguality and position control into the same framework. That matters for understanding whether long-context scaling trends are mostly English artifacts and why lower-resource languages lag more sharply. For pretraining researchers, the benchmark matters less as a ranking tool and more as a failure-surface probe: recency bias, option-order bias, local semantic crowding, and weak grounding in generation become visible as separate phenomena.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, not one that requires line-by-line reading. Start with the introduction and the task overview table to see exactly which variables MGAL controls beyond prior long-context benchmarks. Then read the benchmark construction section carefully: data source, multilingual position alignment, and manual auditing are what determine whether the later claims are trustworthy. In the experiments, focus on results broken down by granularity and position rather than the overall leaderboard. The most useful plots are the degradation curves across discourse units and the begin/middle/end slices. After that, read the qualitative analysis on local semantic crowding and the fluency-consistency gap; those sections contain the most actionable insights for model development. If time is limited, 30–40 minutes should be enough to extract the main value. Prior transfer papers usually evaluate isolated model pairs. UpgradeBench instead measures the full upgrade decision over real release sequences. Third, the paper proposes a more useful task distinction than the usual discriminative vs generative split: some tasks are data-bound, while others are base-bound. t","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-20T02:39:40Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20432.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LO","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的不是“证明是否正确”，而是“通过 Lean kernel 检查的证明，是否达到了 Mathlib 可合入的质量标准”。在形式化数学里，正确性只是底线；真正影响库可维护性和后续复用价值的，是是否善用现有库、是否写出可复用的 lemma、结构是否清楚、是否符合社区约定。论文要解决的是：能否用带工具访问的 LLM judge，稳定地复现 Mathlib 人类 reviewer 对 proof quality 的偏好，而不是只做编译通过/不通过的二元判断。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接贡献不大，但对“可验证环境中的高质量奖励建模”有明确启发。Lean 证明生成近来常用 verifiable reward，只奖励 type-check correctness；这会把模型推向“能过 kernel 但人不想合”的方向。ProofJudge 提供了一个更接近真实维护者偏好的软质量信号，适合作为后续 RL、rejection sampling 或 self-improvement 的奖励源。更重要的是，它说明在工具接地后，LLM 可以读出代码审查里那些难以形式化但又真实存在的标准。不过这篇工作的量级仍偏评测与 judge 设计，不是预训练范式层面的推进。","technical_approach":"方法核心是一个 agentic LLM-as-judge。输入不是单纯的 proof text，而是某个 Mathlib PR 中同一 declaration 的两个版本：早期被要求修改的版本，以及最终被合入的版本。judge 在评估单个版本时并不知道另一个版本的分数，也不知道自己正在做 pairwise 比较，从而避免显式比较带来的提示泄漏。评分 rubric 被拆成五个维度：library leverage、automation fit、structural clarity、statement quality、Mathlib conventions，每个维度打 1 到 10 分，再由 harness 加权成总分。关键设计在于 tool grounding：judge 可以通过 bash 查询 PR 所对应 commit 的 Mathlib 状态，搜索现有定理、查看签名、检查库中已有 API，从而像人类 reviewer 一样判断“你是不是重复造轮子”“这个 lemma 是否应该更一般化”“是否已有一行可调用的现成结果”。这和只靠静态文本 judge 的区别很大，因为 proof quality 很多时候依赖库上下文，而不是 proof token 本身。数据集构造上，作者先用 Claude Sonnet 5 回顾历史 PR，筛出那些 revision 差异显著、且不是纯 linting 或新增 declaration 的样本，再形成 dev split 和 test split。这个筛法有一定人工启发式成分，但能把评测集中在真正涉及 reviewer judgment 的案例上。","experiments":"评测集包含 218 个 declaration pair，另有 123 个 dev pair 用于 rubric 调整。作者测试了 6 个 judge，含 3 个开源权重和 3 个闭源模型，每个模型做 3 次 replicate；每次评估时每个样本最多允许 20 次工具调用，以控制成本。指标是 preference alignment：judge 是否给最终被 Mathlib 接受的 revision 更高分。结果上，所有模型都显著高于 50% 随机基线。最好的是 claude-sonnet-5，达到 80.8%；较弱的 deepseek-v4-flash 也有 63.5%，sign test 的显著性都达到 p < 1e-5。开源模型里，muse-glimmer-30b 为 70.2%，inkling-small 为 69.3%，说明这件事并不完全依赖最强闭源模型。更有价值的是作者对噪声的分析：同一个 judge 在同一批样本上重复运行，约五分之一到接近一半的 verdict 会翻转；某些 ablation 在一次 replicate 上 p=0.34，另一次却是 p=0.006。这说明 judge 方差很大，单次跑分很容易误判方法差异。文中给出的 PR 11640 案例也很说明问题：初版证明重复构造了库里已有结果，修订版直接调用 H.restrictPreimage s；judge 通过搜索库发现已有 declaration，并在 library leverage 维度稳定压低初版分数。这类判断无法靠 lint 或正则完成，必须依赖语义级工具使用。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点看三处。第一是方法部分对五维 rubric 的定义，以及为什么需要 tool grounding；这是整篇工作的真正方法点。第二是结果部分关于 replicate variance 的讨论，这比主表分数更重要，直接关系到以后把 judge 当 reward 是否会导致训练不稳。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about proof correctness per se. R_c；比较 R_v 与 R_c 的描述差异，把差异解释为两类错误：\n- 幻觉：caption 引入了导致检索结果偏离 R_v 的内容；\n- 遗漏：caption 缺少使得 R_c 无法对齐 R_v 的关键细节。\n4) 用 CRS+CQA 产出的结构化“修改建议”驱动 RL 训练：不是只给一个 reward 分数，而是引导模型生成新的候选 caption（更像带外部证据的 refinement），从而缓解“RL 只会在原有候选里挑选”的探索瓶颈。\n与常见做法的区别：\n- 不把 CLIP/VLM 当唯一裁判（单标量 reward），而是用检索集合的一致性作为约束；\n- 不依赖额外标注关键词（对比 SC-Captioner），而是从检索到的相似样本中自举出关键语义；\n- 训练信号更接近“可解释的纠错”，降低 reward hacking Existing certified robustness methods are limited to single-turn inputs; naive multi-turn composition yields bounds that degrade exponentially in the number of turns. We introduce Multi-Turn Certified Robustness (MTCR), a framework that models conversational safety via State-Adversarial MDPs and defines $k$-turn certified robustness as the worst-case safety probability ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T07:43:06Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20820.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究多轮对话中的安全鲁棒性认证：单轮 jailbreak 防御的认证结果不能直接用于多轮场景，因为逐轮相乘的下界会随轮数快速变小，几轮之后几乎没有可用保证。","importance":"这篇更偏安全认证和理论框架，不是预训练方法论文。对基座研究的价值在于提醒两点。第一，多轮对话的风险不是单轮风险的简单重复，历史状态会积累，攻击者会根据模型回复自适应调整输入。第二，安全后训练或 safety tuning 的评测如果只看单轮，很容易高估真实部署稳定性。它对训练目标设计也有间接启发：如果希望模型在长对话中保持安全行为，仅优化单轮拒答或单轮偏好数据不够，需要显式考虑 safety persistence，即安全状态在多步交互中的保持性质。","technical_approach":"作者把多轮对话建模为 State-Adversarial MDP。对话历史经过表示函数映射成状态，攻击者在每一轮从参考输入附近的 epsilon 扰动集合中选择用户消息，模型按策略生成回复，状态随用户输入和模型回复更新。安全性由状态空间上的 Safe predicate 定义。论文定义 k-turn certified robustness：在任意自适应攻击者下，连续 k 轮保持安全的最坏情况概率。直接用单轮认证概率相乘会得到 p^k 的下界，衰减很快。为缓解这个问题，MTCR 引入两类结构。第一是 embedding-space mode decomposition，把状态空间分解为若干 mode，分别认证 mode 内安全性和 mode 间转移，从而比朴素乘法更紧。第二是 (alpha, beta)-safety persistence，刻画安全状态在后续步骤中持续保持的性质；当 beta 大于最坏单轮下界时，认证退化可从 p^k 改善到 beta^k，并可用 1 - k(1 - beta) 给出更可解释的 horizon 估计。论文还给出非重叠分解下的 tightness 结果，以及缺少结构假设时的 impossibility 结论。","experiments":"实验覆盖六个 production LLM。论文报告 MTCR 给出的 certified bounds 在 epsilon-bounded adversary 设定下成立；在静态 epsilon-ball 攻击和 Crescendo-style adaptive attacks 下，经验安全率都高于认证下界，没有观察到违反 bound 的情况。文中给出一个示例说明朴素乘法的问题：如果每轮认证安全概率为 0.95，则 10 轮下界约为 0.60，20 轮约为 0.36，50 轮约为 0.08；如果要求 k 轮安全概率至少 0.9，只能支持约 2 轮。这组数字解释了为什么需要 compositional bounds 和 safety persistence。需要注意，形式化认证只覆盖 epsilon-bounded perturbations；Crescendo 这类语义级攻击主要作为经验测试，不在严格认证威胁模型内。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。优先看 Problem Formulation，弄清 SA-MDP、k-turn certified robustness 和 naive multiplicative bound。然后看 compositional certification 和 (alpha, beta)-safety persistence 的定理陈述，不必逐行读证明。实验部分重点看六个 production LLM 的认证下界与经验攻击成功率之间的关系。对预训练研究员来说，最有用的部分不是具体认证算法，而是它把多轮安全分解成状态转移、模式结构和安全持久性的方式，可用于思考多轮 safety data、RL 训练信号和长对话评测。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses certified robustness for multi-turn LLM safety. Single-turn certificates do not compose well in conversations: multiplying per-turn lower bounds gives guarantees that decay rapidly with the number of turns.","importance":"This is primarily a safety certification paper rather than a pretraining paper. Its relevance to foundation-model research is indirect but useful. It makes clear that multi-turn safety is a sequential problem: conversation history accumulates adversarial influence, and the attacker can adapt after observing model responses. This has implications for safety tuning and evaluation. A model that looks robust in single-turn refusal tests may not preserve safety over longer interactions, so post-training data and evaluation protocols need to account for safety persistence across turns.","technical_approach":"$2a","experiments":"The evaluation covers six production LLMs. The certified bounds are reported for epsilon-bounded adversaries, and empirical safety remains above the bounds under both static epsilon-ball attacks and Crescendo-style adaptive attacks. A motivating calculation shows why naive composition is weak: with a per-turn certified safety probability of 0.95, the lower bound is about 0.60 at 10 turns, 0.36 at 20 turns, and 0.08 at 50 turns; requiring a guarantee of at least 0.9 would allow only around two turns. The formal guarantee is limited to epsilon-bounded perturbations. Semantic multi-turn attacks such as Crescendo are used empirically, but they are not fully covered by the stated certification model.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. We study this problem through intervention timing in multi-party conversation, where an assistant must decide whether to speak or remain silent. This setting exposes class imbalance, asymmetric action costs, and the possibility that exposing reasoning changes the policy being audited. U","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T02:00:21Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20670.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究的是“可弃权行动策略”的 faithful reasoning：当模型需要在‘采取行动’和‘保持沉默’之间做决策时，给出的 reasoning 是否真的参与了决策，而不是事后解释。作者选了一个很具体也很合适的场景——多人对话中的 intervention timing。这里的难点不在生成一句解释，而在于：正例稀少、行动和不行动代价不对称、暴露 CoT 可能改变原本策略、RL 训练还可能让 reasoning 与 action 脱钩。论文要回答的是，在这种 setting 下，reasoning policy、direct policy、SFT、RL 各自会把能力和可审计性推向哪里。","importance":"它不是预训练论文，但和 pretrain/post-train 接口关系很近，尤其是 reasoning 监督、RL 目标设计、faithfulness 审计。对基座研究员有价值的地方有两点。第一，它把“reasoning 是否因果参与决策”从 QA 场景推进到了 abstention policy，这比普通答案生成更接近 agent 真实部署。第二，它给出一个不太讨喜但很重要的结论：更强的决策器未必更可审计，甚至训练得越像 classifier，reasoning 越可能被压扁成无关输出。这个结论对当前大量把 CoT 当作天然可解释接口的做法是个提醒。就贡献量级看，它更像一篇方法论和审计框架论文，而不是带来新训练范式的工作。","technical_approach":"作者围绕 Qwen3-8B 这个 hybrid model 搭建实验：同一底座既可以用 think mode 先生成 reasoning 再决策，也可以用 no-think mode 直接输出 action token。这样可以在尽量不改模型家族的前提下，对比 reasoning policy 和 direct classifier。任务是二分类 intervention timing：给定多人对话前缀，判断 assistant 此刻是否该插话。数据来自约 1.6 万个合成对话，展开成约 17.3 万个 token-level decision points，其中只有约 13% 是应当 intervention 的正例，因此类别极不平衡。训练上，论文比较了几条路线。其一是直接决策式 SFT，让模型学 action token；这通常能得到更强分类器，但 reasoning 会被抑制。其二是保留模型原生 reasoning 的 masked objective，只在模型本来就能做对的样本上训练 reasoning 相关部分；问题是它拿不到模型做错样本的有效学习信号，因此很难超过 base reasoning policy。其三是 think mode 下的 RL，优化准确率或加入 behavioral faithfulness reward。作者指出 group-relative RL 目标在所有 sampled rollouts 都一致时没有梯度，哪怕这些 rollouts 是一致地错，这会让很多“自信但错误”的 prompt 没有学习信号。审计方面，作者用了 activation probes 和行为干预。activation probe 用来检查决策信息在何时变得可解码；行为干预则通过删除、替换 reasoning 的部分内容，观察 action 是否随之变化，以测试 reasoning 的因果作用。论文还专门批评了一些常见但在该 setting 下会误导的指标：只看概率贡献、忽略类别不平衡的 probe accuracy、只在 reasoning 末尾 probing、以及不受控的 flip test。","experiments":"从全文给出的结论看，最强的 action policy 是不显式生成 reasoning 的 direct classifier；而显式 reasoning 的 policy 更容易审计，但准确率更低，主要损失来自 missed interventions，也就是该说话时没说。这个结果很关键，因为它说明能力和可审计性之间存在实打实的张力。训练实验里，decision-token SFT 会进一步强化 direct policy，但同时让 reasoning 更边缘化；masked objective 因为只在模型原本答对的样本上保留 reasoning 监督，几乎无法把 reasoning policy 推到 base model 之上；think-mode RL 也没有显著超过 base reasoning policy，原因不是 RL 无效，而是 group-relative 目标在 rollout 全同意时不给信号，覆盖不到一批最需要纠正的错误样本。审计实验显示，reasoning policy 的最终决策在 CoT 生成过程中逐步变得可由 probe 解码，而 direct classifier 的决策在读完 prompt 时就已经高度可解码，这支持“后者先做决定，前者至少部分在推理中形成决定”的解释。作者还强调单次训练 run 很容易把 seed variance 误判成 reward 设计差异，因此比较不同 RL objective 时必须做多 seed 或 replicate。摘要截断了具体数值，全文片段里也没有完整主表，但从定性结果和实验设计看，作者更关心机制证据而不是单一 leaderboard 数字。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读偏精一点。先看引言里四条贡献，能快速抓住问题边界：它不是一般的 CoT faithfulness，而是 abstaining action policy faithfulness。然后重点看方法部分关于三类训练目标的定义，以及为什么 group-relative RL 在一致错误样本上没有学习信号。再看审计部分，尤其是 activation probe 的时间定位和 reasoning 删除/替换干预；这是全文最有方法含量的地方。最后看作者对评估陷阱的讨论，特别是 class imbalance、单次 run、以及不受控 flip test。预计 30 到 45 分钟能读完关键内容。阅读时建议一直区分两个问题：模型会不会做对，和 reasoning silent, does the exposed reasoning actually participate in the decision, or is it only a post hoc explanation. The testbed is intervention timing in multi-party conversation, which is a good choice because the difficulty is not just generating an explanation. The setting has rare positive actions, asymmetric costs for acting versus abstaining, the possibility that exposing CoT changes the policy being audited, and RL objectives that may further decouple reasoning from action. The central question is how reasoning policies, direct policies, SFT, and RL trade off capability against auditability in this regime.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to the pretrain–post-train interface, especially reasoning supervision, RL objective design, and faithfulness auditing. It matters for foundation-model researchers for two reasons. First, it moves the faithfulness question from QA into abstaining action policies, which is closer to how agents are actually deployed. Second, it delivers an uncomfortable but useful result: the strongest decision policy may be the least auditable one, and training a model into a better classifier can make its reasoning less causally relevant. That is a direct warning against treating chain-of-thought as a naturally faithful interface. In terms of contribution type, this is more a methodology-and-audit paper than a new training paradigm.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"$2d","reading_guide":"This one is worth a more careful skim. The authors call this factual access failure.","importance":"The useful signal for pretraining research is at the pretrain–posttrain boundary. The paper argues that factual degradation after SFT should not be treated uniformly as knowledge erasure. Some failures are wrong-answer generations, but many are expression-level failures such as verbosity, formatting mismatch, or exact-match artifacts. This distinction matters because erased facts require restoration, while retained but inaccessible facts call for controlling distributional drift during adaptation. The idea is relevant to domain adaptation, continual pretraining, and safety-critical SFT settings where preserving broad factual recall matters.","technical_approach":"The work has a diagnostic part and a mitigation part. The diagnostic compares recognition-style evaluation with open-ended recall. Recognition is measured through multiple-choice or candidate-ranking probes; recall is measured through closed-book generation metrics such as EM and F1. The same-fact probes are important because they hold the underlying fact fixed while changing the elicitation format. The mitigation method, Recall-Anchored Distillation (RAD), adds an unlabeled OOD anchoring stream to the usual supervised target-domain loss. For each OOD prefix-continuation example, the frozen base model provides soft next-token distributions on continuation tokens, and the adapted model is trained to match them. This is a distributional anchor rather than answer replay. It does not require OOD labels, rationales, gold answers, or external judges, and it directly constrains drift from the base model’s open-domain continuation behavior.","experiments":"The experiments fine-tune three backbones on MedMCQA. The failure analysis further separates genuine wrong answers from expression failures. RAD is reported to recover OOD factual generation while preserving in-domain gains and outperforming the compared baselines. We propose \\textbf{C}ontinuous \\textbf{L}at\\textbf{E}nt \\textbf{A}dapter \\textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoi","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T16:36:10Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21278.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是安全对齐里的一个老问题：安全性一旦通过 SFT、LoRA、RLHF 这类全局参数更新写进模型，模型会把同一套“更保守”的行为施加到所有输入上，结果是 harmful prompt 的拒答能力上去了，但 benign 或 safety-adjacent prompt 也被一并扰动，出现 over-refusal、推理能力下降、事实性变差。CLEAR 的目标不是再做一个更强的安全 adapter，而是把“是否启用安全行为、启用到什么强度”变成输入条件化的连续控制问题。论文把这个问题落在 latent routing 上：冻结 backbone，只训练一个 safety LoRA 和一个很小的 gate，让模型在有害输入上更像安全模型，在无害输入上尽量保持原模型。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发不在“安全 benchmark 又涨了多少”，而在于它把 post-train alignment 里的 safety–utility trade-off 重新表述成条件化参数干预，而不是单一全局策略。这一点对 pretrain/post-train 接口很重要：如果对齐信号本身是稀疏且输入依赖的，那么把它硬写进全部参数往往会污染原有能力；相反，条件化路由更接近 mixture-of-behavior 的思路。CLEAR 还说明了一件实用的事：不需要外部大 guard model，也不需要重训 backbone，只用不到百万级参数的 gate 就能在 HarmBench 和 utility benchmark 上拿到更好的折中。这类方法对持续对齐、可撤销对齐、按风险分层的训练信号注入都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法结构很克制。第一部分是冻结的 backbone LLM；第二部分是 safety-specific LoRA，只负责学习“遇到危险请求时应如何偏向拒答/安全响应”；第三部分是 latent gate，根据输入的隐藏状态表示输出一个连续分数 g∈[0,1]，控制 LoRA 的注入强度。形式上不是简单地在输出端做 rerank 或加一个分类器，而是直接把门控写进层内权重更新：h'=(W+gΔW)h。这样做的意义是，安全干预不是离散开关，而是连续插值，模型可以在‘完全沿用原模型’和‘充分启用安全 adapter’之间平滑过渡。\n\n论文强调 gate 不是外置 moderation 模块，而是和 safety adapter 联合训练。训练上有两个关键设计。其一是 subtype-aware gate weighting：不同类型的 harmful/benign prompt 在训练 gate 时权重不同，目的是让 gate 不只学到粗糙的二分类，而是对安全相邻样本更敏感，减少 benign prompt 被误判为高风险。其二是 hard pairwise margin objective：显式拉开 harmful 与 benign prompt 在 gating space 里的间隔。这个设计很像把安全路由问题做成表示空间上的排序/间隔学习，而不是只靠 token-level generation loss 间接学出来。相比标准 LoRA，区别不在 adapter 本身，而在 adapter 的激活是 input-conditioned；相比外部 guard + base model 的两阶段方案，区别在于 gate 直接控制生成时的内部表示流，而不是先分类再硬切换模型。\n\n从全文表述看，CLEAR 的核心假设是：安全对齐造成的 utility tax，主要来自“对 benign prompt 施加了不必要的参数偏移”。因此它不追求让安全 adapter 本身更温和，而是尽量让 benign prompt 少经过 adapter。这个假设和方法是对齐的，也解释了为什么作者把 backbone 完全冻结：这样 utility preservation 更容易归因到 routing，而不是归因到更好的微调 recipe。","experiments":"实验主线比较清楚：安全看 HarmBench 和 XSTest，效用看 GSM8K、MMLU、TruthfulQA，底座主要是 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 和 Gemma-2-2B-it。结果上，CLEAR 的卖点不是把某一项能力推到极端，而是在几组基线之间拿到更好的 Pareto 点。\n\n在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上，论文报告 CLEAR 将 HarmBench attack success rate 压到接近 0；与现有安全对齐变体相比，CLEAR 的 ASR 为 0.50%，而其他对齐版本大约在 15.50% 到 24.00% 之间。与此同时，GSM8K 保留到 73.46%，比全局 SFT 和标准 LoRA 高约 7 个百分点。这个数字说明 CLEAR 至少在数学推理这类对安全训练较敏感的任务上，确实减少了 utility tax。\n\n在 Gemma-2-2B-it 上，趋势一致但更能看出小模型受益：GSM8K 从 SFT/LoRA 下的大约 38% 提升到 42%，TruthfulQA MC2 从约 47% 提升到 53%。这说明条件化安全干预对容量更紧张的模型可能更重要，因为小模型更容易被全局安全微调挤占原有能力。\n\nXSTest 的作用主要是看 routing trade-off，即 gate 是否真的学会区分 harmful 与 benign，而不是简单地全局偏高。作者声称用不到 1M 参数的 hidden-state gate 就能得到较好的路由效果，这一点很关键，因为它把 CLEAR 和“再加一个外部安全分类器”区分开了：参数量更小，部署链路更短，也避免了外部模型与主模型失配。\n\n从论文摘要和正文可见的消融重点，应该包括：去掉连续 gate、改成 always-on LoRA；去掉 pairwise margin；去掉 subtype-aware weighting；以及与 SFT、标准 LoRA、已有安全对齐模型的对比。最值得注意的不是绝对分数，而是这些消融是否证明 utility 改善确实来自条件化路由，而不是来自更弱的安全训练强度。如果正文表格里 CLEAR 在相近 ASR 下仍显著保留 GSM8K/TruthfulQA，那这个论点就站得住。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓三件事。第一，看方法图和公式，确认 gate 控制的是层内 LoRA 强度，而不是输出后处理。第二，看训练目标部分，重点找 subtype-aware weighting 和 pairwise margin 是怎么定义的，它们分别解决什么误路由问题。第三，看主结果表，尤其是 HarmBench ASR 与 GSM8K/TruthfulQA 的并列结果，判断它是不是在相近安全水平下保住了效用，而不是单纯放松安全约束。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看 Introduction 里对 alignment tax 的问题设定、方法章节里 gate 的实现与训练目标、以及主实验表和 XSTest 路由分析。预计 20–30 分钟可以形成较完整判断。更值得深挖的点有两个：一是 gate 使用哪一层或哪种 hidden state 作为输入，是否存在层位选择敏感性；二是连续 gate 在生成过程中是 prompt-level 固定、layer-wise 变化，还是 token-wise 动态，这会直接影响它能否处理多轮对话里风险随上下文变化的情况。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"CLEAR targets a specific failure mode in safety alignment: once safety is baked into the model through global updates such as SFT, LoRA, or RLHF-style tuning, the same safer policy is applied to harmful, benign, and merely safety-adjacent prompts. That reduces attack success, but it also perturbs useful behavior on benign inputs, causing over-refusal and utility loss. The paper reframes the problem as conditional intervention rather than stronger global alignment. Instead of asking how to make a safer model everywhere, it asks when and how strongly a safety adapter should be activated for a given input.","importance":"The main value is conceptual and practical. Conceptually, it treats alignment as input-dependent behavior routing, not a single globally modified policy. That matters for the pretrain/post-train interface: if alignment signals are sparse and context-dependent, writing them into all parameters is often a blunt instrument. Practically, CLEAR shows that a small latent gate, under one million parameters, can recover a better safety–utility trade-off without retraining the backbone or adding a large external guard model. This is relevant beyond safety. The same pattern could apply to conditional post-training, reversible alignment, and risk-aware adaptation where one wants to preserve pretrained capability unless intervention is necessary.","technical_approach":"$2e","experiments":"$2f","reading_guide":"$30","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"通过 latent routing 动态激活 safety adapter 来缓解 alignment tax，思路直接且有效，对 post-train 阶段的 safety alignment 有参考价值。","en":"Mitigates the alignment tax by dynamically activating a safety adapter via latent routing. The approach is straightforward and effective, offering valuable insights for safety alignment in the post-train phase."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Stanford University","Computer Science, Stanford University"],"authors":["Chengxiao Wang","Enyi Jiang","Xiaojing Liao","Sanmi Koyejo"],"notable_authors":["Sanmi Koyejo"],"tags":["Safety Alignment","Adapter Routing","Alignment Tax","LoRA"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.21278","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.20492","title":"Annotations as Rollouts: Efficient and Scalable Reinforcement Learning for Video MLLMs","abstract":"Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have become a prevailing paradigm for unified video perception. live inside the model’s conditional distribution itself, rather than only in task machinery such as instruction following, formatting, or answer extraction? To avoid task confounds, the authors study a task-free deterministic readout: the fixed-point structure of the short-window argmax dynamical system x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t). The core problem is whether prefixes systematically alter this fixed-point structure, and whether that effect can be explained by simple prefix-side factors, model-side factors, or a nearby mechanistic account.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, and its direct impact on training recipes is limited. Its value is more conceptual: it pushes prompt sensitivity below benchmark accuracy and into the geometry of the conditional distribution. If a prefix changes fixed-point structure even when there is no task, no sampling noise, and no verbalizer, then at least part of prompt-model interaction is a property of the model’s internal generation dynamics rather than a benchmark artifact. That is relevant to people studying in-context learning, prefix effects, tokenizer/context interfaces, and mechanistic interpretability. For core pretraining work, it is peripheral but intellectually useful.","technical_approach":"$31","experiments":"The main empirical results are about changes in fixed-point fraction and structural class. The authors report that a nine-token prefix can move the fixed-point fraction across most of its range and can change the four-way structural class, while instruction tuning, despite yielding +60.5 IFEval points on a matched pair, changes the fixed-point class by zero. Another key result is the dependence on window size: the fixed-point structure exists only at short windows, and four of six models lose it entirely as the window grows. The paper then tests prefix-side, model-side, and mechanistic explanations, but none survives sample widening. In particular, attention-sink strength agrees in sign with the fixed-point shift for only 2/5 models, which the authors treat as chance-level support. Overall, the experiments read as one robust phenomenon plus a sequence of negative results: prefixes strongly affect short-window conditional dynamics, but the obvious explanatory decompositions do not hold up.","reading_guide":"This is a skim read. Start with the introduction to see why the authors reject task accuracy as the right lens for prompt interaction. Then read the setup carefully: fixed-point fraction, the four structural classes, the 96-start census, and the window sweep are the core of the paper. In the results, prioritize Sections 3 through 6. First understand the size and selectivity of the effect, then see how the prefix-side, model-side, and mechanistic explanations fail one by one. Read with some caution: this is a highly artificial short-window argmax probe, so extrapolation to normal long-context generation should be conservative. Around 30-40 minutes is enough to capture the main idea. The most interesting takeaway is the asymmetry between short prefixes and instruction tuning: the former can strongly alter this readout, while the latter barely moves it, which may hint at what post-training changes and what it leaves untouched.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"视角很刁钻，剥离了具体任务，纯看短窗口的 argmax 动态，发现指令微调不改变底层的读取结构，值得一看实验设计。","en":"A very unique perspective that strips away specific tasks to purely observe short-window argmax dynamics, finding that instruction tuning doesn't change the underlying readout structure; the experimental design is worth a look."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Independent Researcher","Affiliation:Independent Researcher, Chile"],"authors":["Nicolás Vera Zúñiga"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Prompt Interaction","In-Context Learning","Mechanistic Interpretability","Fixed Points"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21315","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.21315","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.20442","title":"Stored in Optimizer State, Valued by Later Training: A Causal Account of Subliminal Trait Transfer","abstract":"Subliminal trait transfer allows a student model to acquire behavioral dispositions from teacher-generated data in which the trait is not semantically expressed. 优化器动量/二阶矩/时钟等），并给出一个精确分解：transport（物理传播）与 valuation（后续路线赋值）。这会影响三类实践判断：\n1) continual pretrain / mid-train：切换数据域或目标时，优化器状态可能携带不可见的历史偏置；\n2) 对齐与去偏：仅重置权重或仅做数据清洗不一定消除行为残留，可能需要处理 moment buffers；\n3) analysis）用在“完整训练器状态”上，推导一个精确的 transport–valuation 恒等式：\n- 前向：用切向量 q_t=\\partial S_t/\\partial \\alpha 跟踪源扰动强度 \\alpha 对训练器状态的影响递推。源扰动以 d_t 形式注入（teacher 诱导的梯度变化），并通过 \\partial G/\\partial S 的反馈项在后续步骤继续影响梯度计算。\n- 后向：从终端行为观测 O（文中在 LLM 上用冻结 prompt bank 的 candidate-string log-likelihood 差分）出发，定义 costate p_T=(\\partial O/\\partial S_T)^\\top 并反向递推，得到每一步的“未来价值” \\lambda_t，刻画“此刻状态扰动对最终行为的边际价值”。\n- 分解含义：transport 是与观测无关的“扰动如何在训练动力系统中存活并传播”；valuation 则依赖未来 continuation（后续训练路线）与 readout（行为测量方式），同一物理痕迹可被赋予不同符号。\n- 因果定位（state surgery）：通过在切点处对训练器状态做移植/置换，识别一阶矩 m_t 是源扰动在移除源数据后继续产生“物理后代（physical descendants）”的主要载体；仅移植一阶矩可在保持当下参数/输出不变的情况下，改变未来更新轨迹；而要恢复终端行为，需要参数 + 一阶矩的组合。","experiments":"从给定正文片段可确认的实验要点：\n- 任务与观测：LLM 实验里终端观测 O 是“目标候选串 vs 参考候选串”的对数似然差，在冻结 prompt bank 上评估，用于量化某种行为倾向。\n- 路线分叉：构造在同一 cut 之后的不同 continuation（文中示例为 neutral vs owl-biased 两臂），用混合梯度端口 g_t^{\\alpha,R} 控制源强度与路线。\n- 预测指标：使用 SSE/ZERO（预测误差相对零预测器误差）衡量 costate/全程预测器是否捕获信号（\\ll 1 表示捕获）。\n- 关键结论：通过 state surgery 复现“载体拓扑”在不同优化器与架构上稳定出现；全视野 costate predictor 能正确预判多种架构下已解析的 route-dependent 行为符号（同一 ancestry 在不同后续训练下出现正/负/近零）。\n具体模型规模、数据规模、提升幅度等数字在提供片段中未出现；需要看正文 §3–§5 与表格（文中提到 Table 3、Figure 1、Appendix M.2）获取。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（60–90 分钟，偏理论但落点清晰）：\n- 先读 §2 Setup and Decomposition：把状态 S_t、切向 q_t、costate p_t、未来价值 \\lambda_t 的定义吃透，这是全文主线。\n- 再读 state surgery 的实验段（文中指向 §3）：重点看他们如何“只移植一阶矩而不改变当下输出”，以及如何判定因果载体。\n- 读 route-dependent sign 的预测实验（§4.2、§5）：关注他们如何构造 matched future forks，以及 costate predictor 的失败/成功边界。\n- 最后翻 Appendix（尤其 subliminal trait transfer persists after the source is removed, and why the same stored trace can later express different (even opposite) behavioral signs depending on the subsequent training route—by identifying the physical carrier in the trainer state and providing a predictive decomposition.","importance":"Highly relevant to pretraining dynamics and continual/mid-training practice. The paper argues that “what training remembers” is not just weights but the full trainer state (weights plus optimizer moments and schedule state). It cleanly separates (i) transport: how a perturbation physically propagates through training, from (ii) valuation: how later training and the chosen behavioral readout assign meaning/sign to that trace. This reframes practical questions like whether to reset optimizer state when switching domains, how hidden biases can survive data removal, and why behavioral effects cannot be attributed to ancestry alone.","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"From the provided full-text excerpt, the experimentally grounded claims include:\n- Behavioral readout: in LLM experiments, O is measured via log-likelihood differences between candidate strings on a frozen prompt bank.\n- Route forking: they construct matched continuations (e.g., neutral vs owl-biased arms) using an affine gradient-port mixture g_t^{\\alpha,R} to control source strength and route.\n- Predictive metric: SSE/ZERO evaluates whether the full-horizon predictor captures the signal (values \\ll 1 indicate success).\n- Main findings: the “carrier topology” (first moment as transporter) replicates across optimizers and architectures; the full-horizon costate predictor correctly anticipates resolved route-dependent sign flips across multiple architectures.\nModel sizes, dataset scales, and exact numeric deltas are not visible in the excerpt; they should be in §3–§5, Figure 1, Table 3, and Appendix M.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (60–90 minutes):\n1) §2 for the formalism: S_t, q_t, p_t, and \\lambda_t. 的表征级探针，用来判断数据配比、去偏数据、对齐训练到底改变了内部机制，还是只改变了输出策略。贡献量级不是新的训练方法，而是一个较清晰的因果测量框架。","technical_approach":"方法把职业偏见拆成两个测量点：内部的用户能力表征，以及最终可观察输出。作者先构造“专家”和“新手”两组职业无关提示，例如“我已经深入研究这个主题很多年”和“我刚开始学习这个主题”，用 difference-in-means 得到 expertise steering vector。具体做法是在某一层取隐藏状态，先对 prompt tokens 求平均，再用专家均值减去新手均值得到方向。给定实际上下文时，取上下文最后一个 token 的 hidden state，投影到该 expertise unit vector 上，得到 expertise score。随后用 steering 实验证明这个方向不只是相关探针，而会因果影响行为：在职业问答中改变回答复杂度，在招聘任务中改变是否录用。数据上，作者构造了 20 个常见职业、每个职业 100 个问题的职业问答集，职业来自美国劳工统计局；招聘任务则基于 Karvonen and Marks 的设置，prompt 包含岗位、简历、姓名、经历和教育背景，要求模型给出
