biases internally even when their observable answers pass behavioral bias tests.","importance":"Its main value for pretraining research is diagnostic. It shows that post-training can suppress biased behavior without necessarily removing the representations learned during pretraining. For base-model teams, this kind of representation-level probe can be useful during pretraining, mid-training, and alignment to separate changes in internal associations from changes in surface response policy. The contribution is not a new training recipe; it is a causal measurement framework for latent bias.","technical_approach":"The method decomposes occupational bias into an internal representation of user competence and an observable output. The authors build an expertise steering vector from two sets of profession-agnostic prompts: expert-like statements such as having studied a topic for years, and novice-like statements such as just starting to learn. At a chosen layer, token representations are averaged per prompt, and the expert mean minus the novice mean defines the expertise direction. For a real context, the expertise score is the scalar projection of the final context-token representation onto the normalized expertise vector. They then test causality by steering along this direction in two settings: a professional QA task and a hiring task. The QA dataset covers 20 frequent occupations from the U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics, with 100 GPT-5-generated questions per occupation. The hiring setup adapts Karvonen and Marks, using prompts with a role, a resume, name, experience, education, and a Yes/No hiring decision.","experiments":"The central empirical result is that the expertise direction mediates behavior. Steering along it changes the technical complexity of answers in the professional QA task and changes hiring rates in the hiring task. The authors then use minimally different prompt pairs to test demographic effects. Holding the rest of the prompt fixed, changing demographic attributes changes how strongly activations align with the expertise direction. A key observation is that surface behavioral metrics, such as reading-level measures, do not always reveal the same differences. This supports the claim that behavioral evaluations can miss latent representational bias. The provided content does not include the full model list, effect sizes, layer ablations, or statistical details, so those numbers should be checked in the experiment tables.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough for most pretraining readers. Focus on the construction of the expertise vector, the definition of the expertise score, and the causal model connecting demographic attributes, competence representation, and output behavior. In the experiments, first check whether steering works across both QA and hiring, then inspect how the demographic prompt pairs are controlled. The appendices with generated QA prompts, hiring templates, and complexity metrics are worth checking because these design choices affect how strongly the conclusions should be read.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 causal setup 和 steering 验证；它提醒对齐评测只看输出会低估预训练表征里的残余偏差。","en":"Read the causal setup and steering tests; it shows why output-only alignment audits can miss residual bias in pretrained representations."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Boston University","Affiliation:Independent Researcher","Affiliation:Boston University"],"authors":["Keren Fuentes","Aaron Mueller"],"notable_authors":["Aaron Mueller"],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","bias","steering-vectors","alignment-evaluation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20347","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.20347","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.20763","title":"CARD: Diagnosing Belief to Action Routing Failures in Vision Language Models","abstract":"Linear probes and activation steering have uncovered that vision-language models (VLMs) internally represent mental states such as agents' beliefs, knowledge, and intentions. However, it is unclear whether and how these representations are used by downstream predictions along these axes. To close this gap, we introduce Cross-Axis Routing Diagnostic (CARD), which steers activations along one axis while measuring the response of a different axis's prediction. Applied to open-weight VLMs on Relay C","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T05:53:48Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20763.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"CARD 关注一个机制诊断问题：VLM 内部即使能线性解码出伙伴的 belief、knowledge、intent 等心理状态，这些表示是否真的被用于后续 action prediction。传统 probe 只能说明表示存在，same-axis steering 只能说明该表示会影响同一轴的回答；它们无法区分“模型没有表示”与“模型有表示但没有把表示路由到动作决策”。","importance":"这篇更接近 VLM 机制分析和评测方法，对预训练的启发在于区分 representation learning 和 representation use。很多多模态预训练或指令数据会提升模型对状态描述的回答能力，但这不等于模型会在行动、规划或协作决策中使用这些状态。CARD 提供了一个较清楚的诊断框架：用跨轴干预检查信息是否从内部表示流向目标预测。对训练目标设计有参考意义，尤其是 VLM/agentic VLM 中仅训练“说出状态”可能不足，需要构造让状态影响行动的监督或 RL 信号。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一，对 belief、intent、knowledge 三个轴分别训练 per-layer、per-head 的 L2 正则 logistic probe，输入是一对互为肯定/否定的 statement，取最终输入 token 的 head activation，得到从负例指向匹配正例的单位方向。第二，选择 held-out accuracy 最高的 top-K=56 个 layer-head 位置，约占全部 heads 的 7%，在 forward hook 中只对最后 token、只对这些 head slice 加入 alpha 倍标准差尺度的 probe direction，alpha 使用 +10 和 -10。第三，不看同一轴输出，而是测量另一个轴的预测变化，例如沿 belief 方向 steering 后观察 action 或 knowledge 的准确率变化。作者用 true-belief 和 false-belief 条件下的 accuracy gap shift，以及 mean-accuracy shift，判断某个内部表示是否被路由到另一个预测轴。Relay Chain 是配套的 cooperative grid-world benchmark，加入了 action axis，用 success 和 counter partition 区分 constant-prior、world-state tracker、belief-conditional 三种行为。","experiments":"实验覆盖四个 open-weight VLM family。关键发现是：belief 表示可以从同一层的 head activation 中线性解码出来，held-out probe accuracy 达到 78% 到 94%；对 belief 方向做 activation steering 后，belief 和 knowledge 预测会出现因果响应，说明这些方向不是纯粹的 probe artifact。但 action prediction 基本不响应 belief steering，表现为 action 相关的 gap-shift 或 mean-accuracy shift 接近零。这个结果指向一种 routing failure：模型编码了合作伙伴的 belief，却没有把该信息稳定用于下一步行动选择。文中还使用正负方向 steering 和 projection-subtraction 作为控制，降低把效果误判为普通输出偏置的风险。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20 到 30 分钟。先看 Method 中 CARD 的定义，尤其是 top-K head selection、alpha=±10 steering、gap-shift 和 mean-accuracy shift 这几个指标。然后看 Relay Chain 的数据构造，确认 success/counter partition 如何区分不同策略。结果部分重点看 3×3 cross-axis matrix：same-axis 是正控制，off-diagonal 才是核心。若只关心预训练启发，可重点关注 belief 可解码但不进入 action 的证据，以及作者如何把这个现象转化为可测的 routing failure。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"CARD asks whether mental-state representations inside VLMs are actually used by downstream predictions. A linear probe can show that a model encodes a partner's belief, knowledge, or intention, and same-axis steering can show that such a direction affects answers on the same axis. Neither test tells whether the belief representation is routed into action prediction. The paper targets this gap: distinguishing absent representations from unused representations.","importance":"The work is mainly a diagnostic and mechanistic evaluation contribution, with a useful implication for multimodal pretraining and agentic VLM training. Improving a model's ability to answer questions about mental states is not the same as making the model use those states when choosing actions. CARD offers a way to test representation use through cross-axis interventions. This is relevant when designing pretraining, SFT, or RL data for cooperative settings: supervision that asks the model to describe beliefs may not train the pathway that turns beliefs into decisions.","technical_approach":"$33","experiments":"The experiments cover four open-weight VLM families. The central result is that belief is linearly decodable from selected head activations with 78% to 94% held-out probe accuracy. Steering along the belief direction causally affects belief and knowledge predictions, which makes the direction more credible than a purely correlational probe. However, action prediction shows little or no response to belief steering: the action-related gap shift and mean-accuracy shift are near zero. This supports the routing-failure interpretation: the model encodes the partner's belief but does not reliably use it when predicting the next cooperative action. The paper also uses opposite-polarity steering and projection-subtraction style controls to reduce the chance that the measured effect is just a generic output bias.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20 to 30 minutes. Start with the CARD method: probe training, top-K head selection, alpha=±10 steering, gap shift, and mean-accuracy shift. Then read the Relay Chain construction to understand why the success and counter partitions are needed. In the results, focus on the 3-by-3 cross-axis matrices. Same-axis entries are positive controls; off-diagonal entries are the main evidence. For pretraining-oriented readers, the most useful part is the evidence that a VLM can encode belief without using it for action, and the way this failure is made measurable through cross-axis activation interventions.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"通过activation steering揭示了VLM内部表征与最终输出之间的断层（有belief但不用），对理解模型内部推理机制和设计更好的对齐/路由策略有启发。","en":"Reveals the disconnect between internal representations and final outputs in VLMs via activation steering (possessing belief but not using it), inspiring understanding of internal reasoning mechanisms and design of better alignment/routing strategies."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Universität Stuttgart","Affiliation:Universität Stuttgart","Affiliation:70569, Stuttgart","Affiliation:Germany"],"authors":["Souptik Kumar Majumdar","Fabian Kögel","Andreas Bulling"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Vision-Language Models","Mechanistic Interpretability","Activation Steering","Theory of Mind"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20763","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.20763","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.20999","title":"Latent Ordinal Evidence, Misaligned Outputs: Inference-Time Ordinal Lens Alignment for Multimodal LLMs","abstract":"Multimodal LLMs apply the language model interface to visual inputs, where ordinal regression tasks such as age estimation, image quality assessment, and disease grading require autoregressive decisions over ordered class labels. We ask whether MLLMs reliably convert internal ordinal evidence into ordered digit-token outputs. Across four ordinal benchmarks and four MLLM backbones, ordinal labels are linearly recoverable from hidden states with Spearman correlation up to 0.938, and a task-designe","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T11:33:22Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20999.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个很具体、但对多模态语言模型接口设计很关键的问题：当任务标签天然有序时，MLLM 内部是否已经形成了可线性读出的 ordinal evidence，而这种证据在最终的自回归 digit-token 输出端却没有被正确暴露出来。作者不是在问模型“会不会做年龄分级/病变分级”，而是在拆开看两件事：一是隐藏状态里有没有顺序结构，二是语言模型的 unembedding 和 digit token 子空间是否把这种结构传到了可生成的答案上。全文的核心结论很明确：内部表示里常常已经有很强的有序信息，但原生数字 token 输出接口会系统性丢失这部分信息，因此生成错误并不一定来自表征不足，而可能来自输出读出层与任务结构失配。","importance":"对基座研究的直接启发不在于 ordinal benchmark 本身，而在于它提供了一个很干净的“latent capability vs output interface”诊断案例。很多时候大家把生成错误归因于 backbone 没学会，但这篇文章说明，至少在一类结构化标签任务上，问题可能出在输出几何：hidden state 中可线性恢复的信号，并没有被词表行空间，尤其是 digit-token row space，有效承接。这个观察对预训练和 tokenizer 设计都有延伸意义。它提示两点：第一，LM 接口并不天然适合所有结构化预测；第二，若任务答案被限制在一个很小的 token 子集上，unembedding 的局部几何可能成为瓶颈。贡献量级偏诊断与方法补丁，不是改变预训练范式的工作，但对理解“模型内部会了，接口没说出来”这类现象很有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法分两部分。第一部分是诊断。作者在四个 ordinal 数据集和四个 MLLM backbone 上，对冻结模型的多层 hidden states 做线性 probe，比较不同 prompt 和不同读出位置下，标签是否可恢复。诊断不只停留在 probe accuracy，还进一步分析 unembedding 矩阵对 ordinal direction 的谱过滤，以及 digit-token 子矩阵 W_S 对该方向的能量保留比例。这个分析把“为什么 probe 能读出来而生成读不出来”具体化成了一个输出子空间问题，而不是泛泛地说 decoding 不稳定。第二部分是 OLA。它是一个 frozen-backbone、inference-time 的校正模块，不改 vision encoder、不改 LM backbone、也不改原始 unembedding。做法是在若干中深层 decoder hidden states 上训练轻量 lens，每个 lens 受 digit-token row space 约束，输出一个层级别的 ordinal distribution；再用学习到的 softmax 权重做跨层融合，得到实例相关的 P_OLA。最后只在答案位置、且只在目标 digit token 集合内，对原始 logits 做 discrepancy-driven correction。这里的关键设计有三点：一是用多层而不是单层，因为 ordinal 结构在中深层逐步形成且不同样本最优层不同；二是 lens 锚定到 W_S，而不是自由 probe，因为目标不是证明可读性，而是把可读性接回原生输出接口；三是 correction 只作用于受限 token 集，避免对开放式生成分布造成大范围扰动。与 hidden steering、offline lens 或 LoRA 微调相比，OLA 的定位更像是输出端对齐，而不是重新训练任务能力。","experiments":"诊断结果是全文最有信息量的部分。作者报告，在“task-designed prompt + last-token hidden state”这个最有效设置下，所有 backbone-dataset 组合的线性可恢复性都不低，SRCC 全部达到 0.756 以上，最高到 0.938；文中点名的峰值包括 Qwen3-VL 在 Adience 上 0.938、Gemma-4 在 Aesthetic 上 0.919、Qwen3-VL 在 HCI 上 0.843。与此同时，digit-token row space 对 ordinal direction 的能量保留在全部 16 个 model-dataset 组合里都低于 1.15%，说明输出端确实严重压缩了这类结构化方向。行为层面，hidden-state 线性 probe 与原生 digit-token 输出之间存在 16 到 77 个绝对点的 accuracy gap，这个差距很大，足以支持“问题主要在读出接口”这一论点。方法比较上，OLA 优于 prompt-only、hidden steering、capacity-matched baseline 和 offline-lens 方案，并且在大多数设置下超过了需要 LoRA 调参的 OrderChain。摘要里没有给出完整逐表数字，但从全文叙述看，作者强调的是：在不改 backbone 的前提下，轻量 lens 训练已经能把一部分潜在 ordinal 能力转化为可用输出。若精读，建议重点核对各数据集上的 ACC、MAE、SRCC 三个指标是否一致改善，以及不同层数 K、是否做 W_S anchoring、是否做 target-restricted correction 的消融。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三处。第一，看 Introduction 和 Task Setup，明确作者不是在做新的 ordinal MLLM，而是在拆 hidden representation 与 output interface 的错位。第二，看 Table 1、Table 2 和 Figure 1，这几处基本给出了全文最关键的证据链：probe 可读、W_S 保留低、行为差距大。第三，看 OLA 方法段，重点理解为什么要做多层 lens 融合，以及为什么 correction 只限制在 digit-token 集合内。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以把主线读清。若想判断它对基座研究有没有更深启发，可以额外看附录里的 per-layer SRCC 曲线和不同 prompt protocol，对“任务提示是否能把内部顺序结构推到最后 token”这个问题会更有感觉。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a precise interface problem in multimodal LLMs: for ordinal tasks, does the model already encode strong ordered evidence in hidden states, while failing to expose it through the native autoregressive digit-token output channel? The key question is not whether MLLMs can solve age grading or disease grading in aggregate, but whether there is a systematic mismatch between latent ordinal structure and the LM output interface. The main finding is clear: ordinal labels are often linearly recoverable from internal representations, yet the unembedding and the digit-token subspace fail to preserve that direction, so generation errors can come from output-side misalignment rather than missing task knowledge.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is diagnostic rather than benchmark-driven. diagnosti","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-05T22:14:59Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20400.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文关注 agentic memory 的一个前置失败：在检索开始之前，预算驱动的 eviction 已经删掉了回答所需的上游前提块。检索算法再强，也只能在剩下的 memory store 中排序，无法找回已经被驱逐的证据。","importance":"这项工作对基座预训练本身不是核心贡献，但对长上下文、agent memory 和外部记忆系统有方法启发。它把问题从“怎么检索相关内容”前移到“哪些内容必须先存活下来”。这个区分很重要，因为长程任务中的关键上下文往往不是和当前 query 最相似的块，而是支撑后续事实或决策的前置条件。对于训练具备长期任务能力的模型，类似信号也可以转化为数据构造和评测设计：不仅考最终检索命中，还要考依赖链是否被保留。","technical_approach":"论文先给出 operational definition。memory 被拆成语义原子块，每个块有确定 token cost；benchmark 提供有向 prerequisite graph，边 (j, i) 表示 block j 依赖 block i。给定 query 和预算 C，retention policy 产生一个 block 排序，再按预算贪心保留。失败模式被定义为 structurally indirect prerequisite eviction：某个上游块与 query 语义相似度弱，但位于回答所需依赖链中；如果它在 retention 阶段被挤掉，后续 retrieval 不可能恢复。方法部分提出 Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection，简称 DSGC。它借鉴 tracing garbage collection：先用 query similarity 选出 query-facing root blocks，再沿已知依赖边向上游传播一跳 liveness，保护直接前提；未获得语义支持或结构覆盖的块更容易被回收。和普通 similarity-based eviction 相比，DSGC 的差异在于 ranking 不只看 query-block 相似度，还显式纳入 prerequisite reachability。","experiments":"实验是确定性 benchmark，带固定 seeds、retrieval-friendly controls、dependency-sensitive targets 和 per-seed trace diagnostics。诊断会把失败分解成被驱逐块、挤占预算的竞争块，以及两者分数 margin。主结果很清楚：在 lexical encoder 下，DSGC 将 full-chain retention 从 0.03 提高到 0.90；在 sentence encoder 下，从 0.23 提高到 1.00。这个结果说明，单纯语义相似度排序会系统性丢掉弱相关但结构必要的上游块，而使用显式依赖边可以显著改善完整依赖链保留率。需要注意的是，benchmark 提供 ground-truth prerequisite edges，因此实验隔离的是 retention 策略，不包含依赖图自动归纳的难度。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 15–25 分钟。先看 Operational Definition，尤其是 block、budget、chain(q)、retention 与 retrieval 的边界定义。然后看 DSGC 方法，重点理解 root set 如何由语义相似度产生，liveness 如何沿 prerequisite graph 传播。实验部分重点看 full-chain retention 指标和 per-seed trace diagnostics；后者比均值更有价值，因为它展示了为什么普通 eviction 会删掉真正需要的前提。若时间有限，可以跳过与已有 RAG 和 memory system 的长背景，只保留 problem formulation、DSGC rule 和 main table。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper isolates a pre-retrieval failure mode in agent memory. Under a fixed memory budget, an eviction policy may remove an upstream prerequisite before retrieval is called. Once that happens, no retriever can rank or recover the missing evidence because it is no longer in the active store.","importance":"The paper is not a core pretraining contribution, but it is relevant to long-context agents and memory-augmented language models. Its useful contribution is the separation between retention and retrieval. Many systems optimize retrieval quality assuming the evidence is still present. This paper shows that weakly query-aligned but structurally necessary context can be lost earlier. That distinction can inform benchmark design for long-horizon models and training data construction for memory-aware agents: success should include preservation of dependency chains, not only retrieval of semantically similar snippets.","technical_approach":"The setup represents memory as atomic semantic blocks, each with a deterministic token cost. A supplied directed prerequisite graph defines dependencies: an edge from block j to block i means i is an immediate prerequisite of j. For a query and a budget, a retention policy ranks blocks and greedily keeps them until the budget is full. The target failure is structurally indirect prerequisite eviction: a block has low direct similarity to the query but is required by the dependency chain needed to answer it. The proposed method, Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection, uses query similarity to select root blocks, then propagates liveness one hop through declared prerequisite edges. Blocks without direct semantic support or structural coverage are treated as easier to evict. Compared with vanilla similarity-based retention, the key change is that reachability through the prerequisite graph affects what survives.","experiments":"The benchmark is deterministic, with fixed seeds, retrieval-friendly controls, dependency-sensitive targets, and per-seed trace diagnostics. The diagnostics identify the displaced prerequisite, the competing block that displaced it, and the score margin between them. In the main suite, DSGC raises full-chain retention from 0.03 to 0.90 with a lexical encoder, and from 0.23 to 1.00 with a sentence encoder. The result supports the paper’s central claim: semantic retention alone can systematically discard prerequisites that are weakly aligned with the query, while even a simple one-hop dependency rule can preserve the complete chain in this controlled setting. A limitation is also clear from the design: prerequisite edges are supplied as ground truth, so the benchmark tests retention under known structure rather than graph induction.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 15–25 minutes. Start with the operational definition, especially the distinction among retention failure, retrieval failure, and reasoning failure. Then read the DSGC rule and check how roots are selected and how liveness propagates over prerequisite edges. In the experiments, focus on the full-chain retention metric and the per-seed trace diagnostics; the traces are the most informative part because they show why similarity-only eviction fails.
